# Project Context

- **Owner:** Stephen Long
- **Project:** Changsha-first Mahjong game built from pwmarcz/autotable, with expanded Chinese rules planned
- **Stack:** .NET 10 backend, EF Core + SQLite initially, optional React + Fluent UI 9 + TypeScript + Vite frontend modernization, single-image Docker deployment
- **Created:** 2026-04-20

## Learnings

- Team initialized with Scribe as Session Logger and Decision Merger.
- Scribe maintains `.squad/decisions.md`, `.squad/decisions/inbox/`, `.squad/log/`, and `.squad/orchestration-log/`.

## Phase I Wave 1 Scribe Sweep — Special-context wins + UX polish (2026-05-22)

**Contribution:** Merged 4-file Phase I Wave 1 inbox + 1 newly-written coordinator translator-gap memo into canonical `.squad/decisions.md` as a single `## Phase I Wave 1 — Special-context wins + UX polish` section with seven subsections. Wrote 1 new coordinator memo (`coordinator-phase-i-wave-1-translator-gap.md`, commit `85c5328`) documenting the SignalR ↔ bundle WS path divergence and the multi-phase carrier pattern applied to `HandResultEntry`. Merged 17 new tests across Vasquez's two suites (9 acceptance + 8 unit), delivering +17 net passes vs Phase H Wave 2 baseline (357 → 374).

**Four-agent orchestration:**

1. **Bishop** (`bishop-phase-i-wave-1.md`, commits `afd59b9` → `7509685` → `9e0439c` → `419ba7a` + test coordination `0117a30`): Five contextual Big Win patterns (天和/地和/海底捞月/河底捞鱼/杠上开花) as new enum values in `WinPattern`. `WinContext` sealed record (5 bool flags) optional on detector. `LastDrawWasKongReplacement` state-flag lifecycle across game-state mutations. State-machine wiring at two Hu-detection sites (`DeclareSelfDrawWin`, `ResolveHuClaim`). WS wire emission for new patterns (camelCase). Cross-lane test coordination: test `HuValidation258` scenario is now canonical EarthlyHand fixture; Bishop owned the assertion update (`0117a30`) due to his rule change causing the drift.

2. **Vasquez** (`vasquez-phase-i-wave-1.md`, commits `b6a512e` → `cd95b5b`): Two test suites — 9-fact acceptance (`SpecialContextWinsTests` driving state machine end-to-end) + 3 facts + 1 Theory ×5 unit layer (`WinPatternTests` pinning contracts). Reflection-defensive symbol probes decouple test compilation from Bishop's commit order. Methodology note: direct state-machine drive (not Runtime) mirrors Wave 2 precedent (`RobbingKongAcceptanceTests`). Coverage: all 5 contextual patterns fire correctly under gating conditions; mutual exclusivity verified; negative cases prevent regressions.

3. **Hicks** (`hicks-phase-i-wave-1.md`, commit `f91c95e`): Score-multiplier breakdown modal + streaming move-log sidebar. Result-modal reads optional `scoreResult` + `allPatterns[]` from wire; move-log subscribes to existing game-state collections. **Critical discovery:** Phase H Wave 2 chip strip was dead code — `PATTERN_LABELS` keyed by PascalCase but `WinPatternToWire` emits camelCase. Phase I Wave 1 fixes via `normalizePatternKey()` + camelCase-first lookup. **Build-command correction:** Wave 2 doc wrongly said `parcel build src/index.ts src/index.html`; `src/index.html` doesn't exist. Corrected to `parcel build index.html …` (TS entry discovered via `<script>` tag; emits single hashed JS). Bundle hashes: JS `74e239e6.js` → `4ce16ecc.js`, CSS `674133df.css` → `8ade01c3.css`. Wave 2 hashes pruned.

4. **Coordinator (acting)** — New memo `coordinator-phase-i-wave-1-translator-gap.md`, commit `85c5328`: Bishop's Phase H Wave 2 detector emits `winResult.allPatterns` + `isRobbedKong` on SignalR path; Hicks's Phase I Wave 1 UI ready to render both. But `ChangshaToAutotableTranslator.BuildHandResult` (bundle WS path) never copied those nested objects — two paths diverged. **Fix:** Extend `HandResultEntry` to carry `WinResult?` + `ScoreResult?`; `BuildHandResult` populates both. **Pattern captured:** Multi-phase architectures need explicit carriers at every boundary (detector → state → scorer → translator) — don't re-run detectors downstream. This pattern emerged from Phase H Wave 2's `AllPatterns` carrier; now applied to translator-layer bridging.

**Gate result:** Phase H Wave 2 baseline **357/0/1** → Phase I Wave 1 final **374 passed / 0 failed / 1 skipped** (+17 net passes from Vasquez's 12 acceptance + 8 unit tests; 1 test reclassified but still passes).

**Inbox files:** All 5 memos present and read (4 agent + 1 coordinator translator-gap new-write); not deleted (`.squad/decisions/inbox/` is gitignored — local-only primary sources per standing instruction).

**Notable:** 
- **Regression prevention:** Hicks's discovery that Phase H Wave 2 chip strip was dead code (PascalCase ↔ camelCase mismatch) surfaced a hidden wire-enum risk. Added to decision memo as regression-prevention rule: always test PascalCase ↔ camelCase keys when enums cross language boundaries (C# → TypeScript). Recommend Phase J adds translator contract test.
- **Build command fix:** Hicks's correction to the parcel build command (removing non-existent `src/index.html`) is a critical invariant for future frontend rebuilds — easy to forget, easy to produce duplicate artifacts. Surfaced prominently in decisions.md build-invariants section.
- **Translator divergence pattern:** Coordinator memo generalized the "carrier at boundaries" pattern from Phase H Wave 2 (`AllPatterns` through state-machine) to Phase I Wave 1 (detector/scorer outputs through translator to WS wire). Multi-phase detectors should document all carriers upfront to prevent future gaps.

**Branch ready for PR against `main`.** All four agents' Phase I Wave 1 work + coordinator translator fix captured in canonical decisions.md.

Documented **validation rules verbatim** (length 64, control chars, case-sensitive, fallback chain) + **Parcel `<input type="text">` stripping gotcha** + **per-game routing source priority** + **cross-lane assertion-flip protocol** + **whitespace-only quirk** for future waves. **Four-agent coordination notes:**

1. **Bishop** (multi-game routing + hydration filter): Removed `DefaultGameId` coercion at two sites; established validated fallback chain (JOIN.gameId → ?gameId query → DefaultGameId). Closed Wave 2 open Q on `WallExhausted` hydration (now excluded). **Key invariant pinned:** `AutotableConnectionManager` lives at bottom of `AutotableWsEndpoint.cs`, not separate file (prevents phantom-file searches in future waves).

- **Protocol-design subsection:** Validated fallback chain (JOIN.gameId → query → default) + source priority + control-char/length rejection + case-sensitivity (Ordinal) all documented verbatim as contracts for future multi-region routing or rule-tightening waves.
- **Cross-agent coordination:** Bishop closes Wave 2 open Q (WallExhausted filter); Hicks discovers Parcel build-time behavior (type stripping); Vasquez flips cross-lane test assertion (protocol shift). All three findings surfaced in decisions.md for team reuse.

## Phase I Wave 4 Scribe Sweep — Proper shanten + spectator + strength tests (2026-05-24)

**Timestamp:** 2026-05-24 (date TBD)
**Branch:** `stlong/phase-i-wave-4-bot-strength-spectator` (all commits pushed; ready for PR)
**Contribution:** Merged 4-file Phase I Wave 4 inbox into canonical `.squad/decisions.md` as a single `## Phase I Wave 4 — Proper shanten + spectator + strength tests` section covering Bishop's rigorous shanten counter + spectator seat backend, Hicks's lobby Spectate UI, Vasquez's 9 tests (3 bot strength + 6 spectator validation), and Coordinator's dead-code resolution (shanten tie-breaker wiring in HardStrategy). Documented **monotonicity property** (loose-tile discard invariant), **benchmark verification** (six hand shapes, < 1 ms each), **Attempt 1 pathology** (seed 40595 4000-step timeout with shanten-primary ordering), **Attempt 2 resolution** (shanten as tie-breaker, minimal change). Phase I Wave 4 result: **402 / 0 / 0 tests** (was 393/0/0 at Phase I Wave 3 → +9 net passes, zero-skip streak 2). Bundle rolled: JS `49eb3789.js` → `c93fbb44.js`, CSS `af973ea2.css` → `3f21032c.css`; Bootstrap hash unchanged. All inbox files remain in place per standing instruction (`.squad/decisions/inbox/` is gitignored — local-only primary sources).

**Four-agent coordination notes:**

1. **Bishop** (shanten counter + spectator backend): Replaced `MinShantenToHu` with rigorous backtracking (Standard path) + SevenPairs formula (two paths, return min). Monotonicity property ensures loose-tile discard never increases shanten. Smoke bench confirmed correctness on six hand shapes (< 1 ms per call). Added `?seat=-1` spectator surface, widened botCount cap to 4 for spectators only, auto-deal one-shot on NEW/JOIN when seat=-1 AND botCount=4. **Key invariant pinned:** ParseSeat range `>= -1 and <= 3` (was `>= 0 and <= 3`); spectator → ViewerSeat `null` (existing privacy filter falls through). Initial bench caught formula bug (extraneous `+1-pair` term) before commit — caught via monotonicity verification table.

2. **Hicks** (lobby Spectate UI): New `Seat` fieldset (Auto / 0..3 / Spectate) above Bot difficulty. Spectate selection unlocks 4-bot slot (disabled for non-spectators) + pre-selects 4 on flip from non-spectator. Green "Spectating" pill on connected state. URL `?seat=-1` persisted via `history.replaceState`. Spectator mode hides Take-seat / Leave-seat / Claim buttons / Deal (server auto-deals) / Pickup HUD; bot banner stays (names seating). **Critical build invariant reaffirmed:** Parcel strips `type="text"` from `<input>` — anchor CSS on ID/scoped class, not attribute selector (surfaced in Wave 3, applies to new `.spectator-pill`).

3. **Vasquez** (9 tests, no production edits): 3 `BotStrengthTests.cs` (Hard beats Medium ≥ 0.9×, Medium beats Easy ≥ 0.9×, Hard no-draw regression) + 6 `SpectatorModeTests.cs` (connects without seat, receives full snapshot, no turn prompts, 4-bot auto-deal within 3s, 3-bot no auto-deal, seat=0 botCount=4 defensive assertion). Audit flagged dead code: Bishop's rigorous counter delivered but never consumed by HardStrategy. Fixed by Coordinator.

4. **Coordinator** (dead-code resolution): Vasquez audit surfaced `MinShantenToHu` was orphaned. Attempt 1 (shanten as primary key) broke at seed 40595 with 4000-step timeout — pathological claim-chain loop (state-machine edge case, not bot bug; deferred to Phase J). Attempt 2 shipped: `OrderBy(ComputeDiscardScore).ThenBy(shantenByLogical)` — keep-score primary (Phase F baseline), shanten tie-breaker (minimum change). Result 402/0/0. Rationale: Changsha's Big Win mix favors defensive/contextual plays; keep-score was already stronger than shanten-greedy. Promoting shanten demands re-tuning every Hard heuristic; tie-breaker exercises counter in production without disturbing baseline.

**Notable observations:**
- **Shanten-as-tie-breaker pattern:** When a rigorous estimator (counter, evaluator, heuristic) is delivered but not yet integrated, shipping it as a tie-breaker (not primary sort key) is the minimal, lowest-risk wiring that exercises it in production. Allows future phases to promote it to primary after understanding its interaction surface more deeply.
- **Benchmark-caught bug:** Initial formula error (`+1-pair` term) inflated shanten by 1 for any decomposition without a pair. Monotonicity verification table (discard → shanten delta) caught it immediately; removing the term restored canonical values across all six test cases + kept gate at 402/0/0. Recommends running perf + property-based benches on backtracking algorithms during initial implementation.
- **Dead-code audit pattern:** Phase I Wave 4 Vasquez audit (`MinShantenToHu` never called) triggered Coordinator memo and resolution in same wave. When audit surfaces unused production code, surface it in the same wave's decisions.md + resolution memo, don't defer. Enables immediate re-integration or cleanup decision.
- **Zero-skip streak milestone:** Wave 4 maintains zero-skips (357 → 374 → 383 → 393 → 402). Consecutive zero-skip waves indicate test suite stability + low flake risk.

**Phase J Wave 1 backlog:** Diagnose seed 40595 4000-step pathology (trace harness needed), promote shanten to primary key (demands re-tuning keep-score weights), wire shanten into OnDiscardOpportunity (claim evaluation), hot-seat swap UI ("Move" button), NineTerminals strict-vs-loose semantics (pending Stephen's call).

**Branch ready for PR against `main`.** All four agents' Phase I Wave 4 work + Scribe sweep captured in canonical decisions.md.

**Contribution:** Merged 3-file Phase J Wave 1 inbox into canonical `.squad/decisions.md` as a single `## Phase J Wave 1 — Shanten claim gate + hot-seat swap + spectator camera lock` section covering Bishop's shanten-aware claim gate + wall-exhaustion fast-path deferral, Hicks's Move button + spectator camera lock, and Vasquez's 7 new test facts (4 claim evaluator + 3 hot-seat swap). Documented **Kong-over-Pung dead-code reality** (mathematically unreachable via realistic adjudicator output; reframed Fact 4 to Pung-vs-Chow instead), **Task 2 deferral rationale** (state machine already short-circuits on empty wall; adding runtime check would be inert), **autotable disconnect seat-release gap** (SignalR Hub only; Bundle UI works around by disabling current seat). Phase J Wave 1 result: **409 / 0 / 0 tests** (was 402/0/0 at Phase I Wave 4 → +7 net passes, zero-skip streak 3). Bundle rolled: JS `c93fbb44.js` → `214d524e.js`, CSS `3f21032c.css` → `884bb475.css`; Bootstrap hash unchanged. All inbox files remain in place per standing instruction (`.squad/decisions/inbox/` is gitignored — local-only primary sources).

**Three-agent coordination notes:**

1. **Bishop** (shanten claim gate + Task 2 deferral): Wired `MinShantenToHu` into `DecideClaimPhase` as strict-shanten-drop gate for non-Hu opportunities. Contract: Hu unconditional (fast-path); non-Hu only if post-claim shanten < pre-claim. Tie-breaker rank Hu > Kong > Pung > Chow. Helpers ShantenAfterPungClaim / ShantenAfterExposedKongClaim / ShantenAfterChowClaim simulate post-claim hand; ClaimAcceptanceRank encodes ordering. Chow simulation mirrors `RemoveChowTilesByLowestPattern` first-viable-pattern selection (lowest-rank-first) so gate decision matches runtime play. Resolves Phase I Wave 4 Vasquez audit (dead code). Deferred Task 2 (wall-exhaustion fast-path) with clear reasoning: `ChangshaGameStateMachine.AdvanceToNextPlayer:1076-1087` already checks `Wall.Count == 0` before setting AwaitingDiscard; both call sites (Discard / PassClaim) route to WallExhausted on empty wall. Adding duplicate check in runtime driver would be functionally inert + risk dropping wall-exhausted event broadcast. Per wave brief, SKIP.

2. **Hicks** (Move button + camera lock): New sidebar row with Move button + seat picker (Seat 0..3 + Spectate). Visibility: `connected() && match.get(0) === null` (disappears post-Deal). Picker per-option disabled state: current seat + occupied seats. Soft reconnect: `history.replaceState` rewrite `?seat=`, clear local seats entry (avoid stale reapply), `client.disconnect()`. Auto-reconnect picks up new seat via existing `buildWsUrl()`; body class + spectator pill sync automatically. Spectator camera lock: one-liner in `world.ts` initializing `seat` field from `readSpectatorFromUrl()` instead of hard-coded 0, eliminating seat-0 first-person flash. `main-view.ts` untouched (existing `fromTop` branch handles null seat already). No orbit-controls to disable. Files: `index.html` / `game-ui.ts` / `style.css` / `world.ts` + bundle roll.

3. **Vasquez** (7 new tests, zero skips): Two suites — `ClaimEvaluatorTests.cs` (4 facts pinning Bishop's gate) + `HotSeatSwapTests.cs` (3 facts on swap surface). All facts reflection-defensive (symbol probes stay compile-stable across refactors). Claim facts: refuse-on-shanten-rise (SevenPairs-candidate hand) / accept-on-shanten-drop (chow-partial-rich) / unconditional-Hu / tie-breaker-rank (reframed Pung-vs-Chow per Kong dead-code discovery). Hot-seat facts: player↔player binding swap preserves runtimeGameId / player→spectator "does-not-claim-seat" (not "frees" — disconnect doesn't call HandleDisconnectAsync) / spectator→player binds-seat. All 7 run unconditionally (no Skip).

**Notable observations:**
- **Kong-over-Pung dead-code discovery:** Shanten counter treats any concealed 3-of-a-kind as complete pung. Kong from discard moves that "group" to declared meld (zero net gain); Pung removes 2 (leaves dangler, usually worse shanten). So both Kong and Pung cannot strictly drop shanten simultaneously on same tile. Bishop's `ClaimAcceptanceRank` lift (Kong=3 > Pung=2) is defensible defence-in-depth (matches runtime's CCW preference) but mathematically unreachable. Vasquez discovered this during Fact 4 construction, reframed to Pung-vs-Chow (both drop shanten, rank decides). Pattern: realistic-adjudicator-output verification early in test-first design catches theoretical-but-unreachable code paths.
- **Autotable WS disconnect gap:** Only SignalR Hub path calls `HandleDisconnectAsync` to release runtime seats. Autotable path leaves seats occupied. Bundle UI workaround: disable current seat in picker. Recommendation: Phase J Wave 2 brief if seat-release becomes priority (larger refactor impact).
- **Task 2 deferral discipline:** Wave brief criterion ("if you can't make the change cleanly without risk to existing test behaviour, SKIP THIS TASK") applied. State-machine already has guard; runtime guard would be inert. Rather than add cosmetic code + risk event broadcast change, explicitly document deferral + move to backlog. Clear reasoning in decisions.md prevents future "why wasn't this done?" questions.
- **Zero-skip streak (3 waves):** I-W3, I-W4, J-W1 all 0 skips (357 → 374 → 383 → 393 → 402 → 409). Consecutive streak indicates stable test suite + low regression risk across bot logic + UI + protocol changes.

**Open questions for Phase J Wave 2:** Autotable WS seat-release (gap documented), HardStrategy.OnTurnStart Win-context audit, i18n AllPatterns ordering, NineTerminals semantics, per-game _bindingLock profiling, Seed 40595 4000-step pathology (from Phase I Wave 4 backlog).

**Branch ready for PR against `main`.** All three agents' Phase J Wave 1 work + Scribe sweep captured in canonical decisions.md.

**Four-agent coordination notes:**

Legacy `EndGame` also sets `IsGameComplete=true`. Runtime fires both `GameEnded` (backward compat) + new `GameCompleted` event (payload: gameId, hand, maxHands, finalScores, winner, phase). Hydration filter widened to skip both terminals. Audit: WinContext-less bot probe correct (context layers bonus flags only; no flag promotes non-winning to winning). Test setups: 3 legacy tests raised `MaxHands=100` per wave brief authorization.

2. **Hicks** (end-of-game modal + reconnect banner + settings drawer): End-of-game summary modal triggered by `IsGameComplete`; per-seat totals table (sorted by score, local player gold "(You)") + hand-by-hand recap + "New Game" (URL rewrite + reload) + "Back to Lobby" buttons. Defensive payload parser accepts camelCase/PascalCase completion-flag variants + optional totalScores/handHistory/maxHands fields. Connection-lost banner replaces silent reconnect: exponential backoff 1/2/4/8/16s × 5 attempts (yellow→red→green states); user-initiated disconnect silent; auto-reconnect shows banner. Settings drawer (gear ⚙): Bot Strength (Easy/Medium/Hard, default Hard) / Hand Count (1/4/8/16, default 4) / Auto-Deal checkbox; gameId-keyed localStorage + global fallback; read priority URL > localStorage > defaults. Lobby defaults shifted: handCount 8→4, botDifficulty Medium→Hard (parity with runtime default). (2) GameComplete vs EndGame overlap; both set IsGameComplete=true; future features branching on Phase directly should handle both. (3) Second consecutive wave where Bishop's hand-counter changes broke legacy tests via MaxHands=100 workaround; recommend J-W3 sweep audit.

4. **Stephen Long** (standing directive): "No pauses — quit asking. Keep iterating to 100% done. Fan out and get the team working. Pre-approved team-size expansion if scope demands." **Notable observations:**
- **HotSeatSwap assertion flip contract:** J-W1 test author's forward-looking comment ("if a future wave promotes seat-release to autotable, flip this assertion") created explicit contract for J-W2. When such comments appear, honor them during the anticipated future wave — don't require re-review. Vasquez's Phase J Wave 2 memo explicitly pre-authorised this as the only test mutation under "DO NOT touch tests" rule.
- **MaxHands=100 test-setup pattern recurring:** Second consecutive wave (I-W4 also had similar issue). Pattern: when production contract introduces a new default parameter, ensure all legacy tests that assumed the old default are explicitly opted-out via new parameter in their setup. This prevents silent breakage on future refactors. Recommend audit of all *HandTests + *BankerRotationTests files if J-W3 adds more hand-counter contract changes.
- **WinContext audit resolved:** Bishop's audit finding from J-W1 memo ("validate any-win context derives correctly in all paths") addressed: context-less detection in bot probes is correct — context only layers bonus flags onto already-winning hands. Canonical context builders (DeclareSelfDrawWin / ResolveHuClaim) are sole sources of truth; all scoring paths flow through them. No code change needed; documented for future auditors.
- **GameComplete vs EndGame distinction:** New `GameComplete` phase (N-hand cap) vs legacy `EndGame` (4-round / 16-hand terminal) now coexist. Both set `IsGameComplete=true` for phase-agnostic callers (Hicks's modal). Future features branching on Phase directly must handle both. Documented in decisions.md.
- **Zero-skip streak (4 waves):** I-W3, I-W4, J-W1, J-W2 all 0 skips (357 → 374 → 383 → 393 → 402 → 409 → 418). Four-wave consecutive run confirms test suite stability across bot tuning + claim gate + disconnect handling + UI overhaul + game-completion protocol changes.
- **Standing directive captured:** Stephen's "no pauses" directive now canonicalized in decisions.md as formalized protocol, enabling autonomy across coordinator + Scribe transitions between waves.

**Notable observations:**
- **Author-hygiene WIN extended to 4/4 for the second consecutive wave.** Phase J Wave 10 was the first 4/4 PERFECT wave (each agent self-configured `git config user.name` before committing, no cross-lane file bundling, no coordinator patches needed). Phase K Wave 1 held the line: Bishop owns backend + 3× EF migrations + appsettings tweaks; Hicks owns `history.ts` + `tour.ts` + `index.html` + `index.ts` + `leaderboard.ts` + `tournaments.ts` + Parcel rebuild + `tests/selectors.md` footer; Apone owns 3 new workflows + 2 smoke scripts + CHANGELOG + 2 new docs (`image-signing.md`, `secret-rotation.md`); Vasquez owns 15 new test files + the renamed `Wave1ThroughKRegressionTests.cs` + 6 Playwright specs. Pattern carries into Wave 2 as the established baseline.
- **Bishop's `IOptions<AuthOptions>` DI fix is a forward-applicable lesson.** Pre-Wave-K `AuthOptions` was registered as a bare singleton only. New services (`OAuthStateProtector`, `OAuthProviderHealthCheck`) take `IOptions<AuthOptions>`; both binding paths are now registered side-by-side. Future services consuming `AuthOptions` should prefer the `IOptions<>` path.
- **Singleton-when-you-own-a-`IServiceScopeFactory` pattern reaffirmed.** Bishop's `PlayerRatingService` moved Scoped → Singleton mid-wave because integration tests resolve from `Factory.Services` (root provider) — scope-validation throws on Scoped services that don't actually need scope. Mirrors `TournamentService` / `MatchmakingService` / `PlayerProfileService`.
- **OAuth state-cookie semantics flipped this wave.** Pre-Wave-K the `mahjong_oauth_state` cookie held the opaque state token directly; Wave-K splits responsibility: the **token** travels in `?state=` (HMAC self-validates) and the **cookie** holds only the embedded nonce (cookie-binds the redirect). Two new cookies (`mahjong_oauth_pkce`, `mahjong_oauth_nonce`) round out the PKCE + OIDC-nonce flow. **Any future third OAuth flow MUST mint all three cookies.**
- **Vasquez caught the multi-arch YAML regex bug mid-wave.** First filtered run reported one fail (regex didn't allow sibling `fail-fast:` between `strategy:` and `matrix:`); fixed in Vasquez's own lane with a more permissive pattern + widened the per-arch `--platform=linux/...` check to also accept the space-separated CLI form `--platform linux/...`. Pattern: forward-staged QA + first-attempt-bind discipline lets contract tests act as a real-time linter on the other agents' artefacts.
- **Lobby <500 kB target NOT met this wave** (Hicks shipped 1.318 MB main vs Wave-10's 1.275 MB; the new K-1 code is ~96 kB total of which ~53 kB peeled into lazy chunks). Reaching <500 kB requires extracting `Game` / `World` / `three` / `Client` from the lobby's eager graph — invasive, deserves its own wave. Documented as a Wave 2 follow-up.
- **CSP strict-styles production flip is gated on Bishop's `appsettings.Production.json` edit.** Apone's `multi-arch-smoke.yml` already runs the image with `Security__CspStrictStyles=true` to prove the binary supports the knob on both arches; once Bishop commits the production overlay flip, prod CSP tightens automatically. Documented in the decisions.md hand-off list.
- **Zero-skip streak extended to 15 consecutive waves** (J.1 → J.10 + K.1). No wave in this run has added a skip — six (Phase I Waves 3-4 plus Phase J Waves 1-10) before this one plus K.1 now. Quality-metric continuity sustained across bot logic + claim-gate + protocol changes + multiplayer + observability + auth + tournament mode + production bring-up.

**Inbox content worth flagging to coordinator for the PR body:**
- **Bishop's `OAuthStateProtector` rejected-Reason taxonomy** is a clean operator-visible audit signal (`length`, `format`, `expiry`, `hmac-mismatch`); worth surfacing in the PR description as a security-review highlight.
- **Apone's cosign verify regex** anchors at the workflow path (`.../sign-image.yml@refs/(heads/main|tags/v.*)$`) — renaming the workflow breaks every verifier. PR reviewers should not rename `sign-image.yml` casually.
- **Hicks's lobby bundle size note** is a known regression vs the long-term <500 kB target and is best called out in the PR body so reviewers don't flag it as an unintended size jump. The lazy-load chunks (~53 kB peeled) are still a clear win for sessions that don't open chat/tournaments/tour.
- **Vasquez's blind-spot list** (8 items, from OAuth live-discovery integration lane to tour first-launch server-side detection) seeds Wave 2 backlog directly — coordinator should pull these into the Wave 2 brief.

**Notable observations:**
- **Cross-lane forward-staging codified.** Bishop's two intentional cross-lane edits (`5a845cb` Vasquez tests + regression rename; `636329e` `infra/k8s/base/kustomization.yaml` wires `turn-server.yaml` into the Apone-lane kustomization) establish the canonical pattern for when cross-lane edits are allowed: (a) in-commit message must explain the scope crossover AND (b) BOTH the staging agent and the authoring agent must cross-reference the edit in their inbox memos. Without that dual-signature, future cross-lane edits get bounced at PR review. Documented prominently in the Wave-2 decisions.md section + reaffirmed in the standing-directives subsection.
- **Bundle-budget win is the new baseline.** Hicks closed Wave 1's 1.318 MB eager bundle at **208.44 kB** (−84 %) by splitting the renderer chain into `game-bootstrap.<hash>.js`. The lazy-chunk size table captured in decisions.md (8 chunks: bootstrap 1.11 MB / Sentry 395 kB / tournaments 23.8 kB / history 12.3 kB / chat 12.2 kB / tour 9.5 kB / audit 7.4 kB / voice 5.6 kB) is now the canonical Wave-3 budget reference. New Wave-3 budget rule: any new top-level import that pushes `autotable-src.<hash>.js` over 250 kB MUST be lazy-loaded from `game-bootstrap.ts` (or later). One-liner check: `ls -lS ../autotable/autotable-src.*.js | head -1`.
- **`dom-utils.ts` vs `utils.ts` purity split is the seam.** Pre-Wave-2 `utils.ts` re-imported `Vector3` / `Quaternion` at module top-level for `SEAT_ROTATIONS`, which pulled three.js into every module that imported a single DOM helper. The split (pure DOM in `dom-utils.ts`; three.js-bound geometry in `utils.ts`) is what unlocked the −84 % win. Future contributors: import DOM helpers from `./dom-utils` directly, not via `./utils` (which still re-exports them as a back-compat shim).
- **Audit `Kind` taxonomy is dotted-string.** `reconnect.token.rotated` / `tournament.forfeit` / `tournament.match.complete` / `voice.join` / `voice.leave`. Future audit kinds MUST follow `domain.event` shape; the `IX_ReconnectAuditEntries_Kind_At` composite index assumes string-comparable kinds. Replaces the Wave-1 synthetic `PlayerId == "tournament-forfeit"` hack.
- **OAuth discovery cache TTL hierarchy locked.** `CacheTtlSeconds = 21600` (6 h) → `RefreshIntervalHours = 6` → `StaleThresholdHours = 24`. Two knobs (`Authentication:HealthCheck:SkipDiscovery` for the Wave-1 health-check probe + `Authentication:Discovery:SkipNetwork` for the Wave-2 discovery cache) serve different surfaces and toggle independently — air-gapped CI needs BOTH set to `true`.
- **VoiceHub publicly mappable without auth.** Wave 2 ships the hub without per-table membership auth; this is captured as a Wave-3 / Phase-L follow-up in both decisions.md and `.work/known-limitations.md`. A stranger could theoretically broadcast SDP into a tournament room today — mitigated only by needing to know the table ID. Wrap against `AuthCookieService` in Wave 3.
- **PR-time multi-arch runtime gate vs. post-merge smoke factoring.** Apone deliberately kept `multi-arch-runtime.yml` (PR-time, local build against PR commit) and `multi-arch-smoke.yml` (Wave-1, post-merge, against the PUBLISHED image) as TWO workflows. Combining muddies both; the trigger shape + image source are different. Sticky PR comment via `marocchino/sticky-pull-request-comment@v2` with stable `header:` is the canonical pattern for matrix-verdict surfacing on PRs.
- **Reusable cosign verify workflow.** ONE source of truth for the expected-identity regex + cosign version. Today only `release.yml` consumes it; tomorrow Argo CD pre-sync gates + Kyverno admission policy will. Renaming `sign-image.yml` later means updating ONE consumer.
- **Unique smoke port allocation extended to 18093.** docker-build=18080, auth=18081, chat=18082, token-rotation=18083, csp-report=18084, multi-arch-runtime(amd64)=18091/(arm64)=18092, **pwa=18093**. Allocate the NEXT free port for any new smoke and document in the wrapper header.
- **Zero-skip streak extended to 16 consecutive waves** (J.1 → J.10 + K.1 + K.2). No wave in this run has added a skip. Quality-metric continuity sustained across production bring-up → production deepening — every Wave-2 surface (7 backend + 1 DevOps + 6 frontend + 8 contract files + 6 e2e specs) binds via Vasquez's reflection-defensive 3-shape pattern (`Type.GetType(...) is null → return`, assembly-scan + `FirstOrDefault → return`, route-probe + `404 → return`).
- **4/4 author-hygiene WIN extended to THREE consecutive waves.** J.10 was the first 4/4 perfect wave; K.1 held; K.2 holds again. Each agent self-configured `git config user.name "<Agent>"` before committing, no cross-lane file bundling EXCEPT the two formally-documented forward-staging exceptions (5a845cb / 636329e). Pattern is the established baseline going into Wave 3.

- **`VoiceHub` is `HubException`-free.** Every RPC now returns `Task<VoiceHubResult>` (`{ Ok, Reason }` typed-record); rejections increment per-reason counters (`RecordRateLimitRejection`, `RecordJoinUnauthorized`); frontend `voiceReasonToText()` mapper translates all 6 W4 codes (`voice-not-enabled`/`not-seated`/`spectator`/`rate-limited`/`target-not-found`/`unauthorized`) to user-facing copy. Wire shape is forward-compatible with the Wave-3 string-reason fallback via `readVoiceResult()`.
- **Scene chunk three-tier hierarchy.** `scene-shell` 886 kB (renderer-critical: three.js + AssetLoader + Game + World + ClientUi) + `scene-effects` 60 kB (deferred: GameUi + MoveLog dynamic-imported after first frame) + `game-state` 1.9 kB (singleton cache lazy-imported by voice + settings-drawer, live-pushed via SignalR `GameJoined`). Total bytes on game URL +2.4 % vs Wave 3 but ~−40 kB on the renderer-critical first-paint chain. `scene-shell` 500 kB target NOT met this wave — three.js alone is ~575 kB minified; Wave 5 follow-up lazy-imports three itself into a third chunk.
- **Four-layer supply-chain enforcement complete with SLSA L3.** Workflow signer (`sign-image.yml`) → release verify-gate (`verify-signature.yml` in `release.yml`) → cluster admission (Kyverno W3 `verify-mahjong-images` + W4 `enforce-prod-mahjong-images`) → SLSA L3 in-toto provenance (`slsa-provenance.yml` calling the official `slsa-github-generator` reusable workflow on a SEPARATE isolated runner pool — the L3 non-falsifiable guarantee). Plus the Wave-3 SBOM signing chain. Signer-identity regex is the cross-layer invariant — duplicated across FIVE locations now (W3 had three).
- **Two-policy Kyverno prod hard-pin.** Apone shipped `enforce-prod-mahjong-images` as a SEPARATE ClusterPolicy under `resources:` (not a patch on the Wave-3 `verify-mahjong-images` — that would have flipped staging to Enforce too, breaking audit-only experimentation). Multiple Kyverno policies on the same image compose; either failing rejects. Resilient to a misedit of the global default. Vasquez's `KyvernoEnforcePatchContractTests` uses the "either-form contract probe" pattern to accept BOTH shapes.
- **HSTS preload at the ingress layer** (NOT in the C# `SecurityHeadersMiddleware`) so operators probing the header BEFORE submitting to hstspreload.org see the wire-level value, and so future middleware refactors cannot silently weaken the header (chromium preload pins are months-to-undo).
- **Defense-in-depth secrets scanning.** GitGuardian SaaS (push events, README-recommended) + gitleaks in-CI (`secrets-scan.yml` on PR diff + nightly cron at 03:00 UTC; ruleset pinned at the action version so it can't drift silently). Two layers, two failure modes; SARIF categories distinct so findings don't overlay.
- **Vasquez's reflection-defensive pattern grew three refinements** in Wave 4: (1) reflection-async unwrap (`Task<T>`-returning methods via `mi.Invoke` → cast to `Task`, `await`, then read `.Result` property — avoids xUnit1031 blocking calls); (2) HTTP precedence via dev-login (`POST /api/auth/dev-login` with `{ email, displayName, role }` mints role-distinct cookie sessions for testing the 401→403→404→400 chain); (3) either-form contract probe (accept EITHER shape when the agent-of-record ships a different surface than initially specced — used for Kyverno patch-vs-policy and `Auth:` vs `Authentication:` JWT config keys).
- **`MaxParallelThreads=2` is still the canonical Wave-4 closeout invocation.** Hudson's per-class `CollectionFixture` work (per `docs/test-harness-handoff.md`) is the long-term fix; once it lands in Wave 5, default xUnit parallelism resumes.
- **4/4 author hygiene this wave** (Bishop, Hicks, Apone, Vasquez all configured cleanly). Wave-4 prompt-template preamble carried the remediation forward from Wave 3 — and it worked.

**Inbox content worth flagging to coordinator for the PR body (top 5):**
1. **Bishop's `VoiceHubResult` typed-record refactor — no more `HubException`.** Every `VoiceHub` RPC now returns `Task<VoiceHubResult>` (`{ Ok, Reason }`) with stable `Reason*` constants on the server side and a `voiceReasonToText()` mapper on the client (Hicks). The wire shape is forward-compatible with Wave-3 string-reason via `readVoiceResult()`. Closes the cross-team typed-result hand-off from Wave 3 (item #8 of Bishop's W4 list, item #5 of Hicks's W4 list). Pairs with new `RecordRateLimitRejection` + `RecordJoinUnauthorized` counters on `VoiceHubMetricsService`.
2. **JWT signing-key data plane end-to-end.** Bishop's `Auth.JwtSigningKeys` array binding + deterministic `kid` header + `POST /api/auth/token` (admin-mint) + `POST /api/auth/validate` (100/min/IP) + manual HS256 RFC-7519 mint (no `Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens` transitive surface) + Apone's ESO `mahjong-jwt-keys` ExternalSecret + 3 rotation-state-named SSM SecureString parameters + Vasquez's kid-rollover contract suite (9 facts) close the entire rotation surface this wave. `docs/jwt-rotation.md` is now the single source of truth.

Completes the four-layer supply-chain enforcement chain (workflow → release gate → cluster admission → SLSA provenance) — the single most significant security posture upgrade of the wave.

Unified `game-state.ts` reactive cache (1.9 kB) eliminates duplicate `/api/games/{id}/settings` fetches and live-pushes ownerId + voiceEnabled flips via SignalR `GameJoined`. `scene-shell` did NOT hit the 500 kB target (three.js is 575 kB alone); logged as Wave-5 lazy-three follow-up. ~−40 kB on the renderer-critical first-paint chain.

The Wave-4 author-hygiene preamble worked at the git-author level (4/4 author hygiene, 11/11 commits with correct authors) but did NOT prevent cross-agent work-content bundling. Wave 5 prompt template adds `git stash --include-untracked -m "<name>-w<N>-uncommitted"` discipline before any other agent could pick up the work, then `git stash pop` immediately before each agent's first commit. PR reviewers should NOT bounce these — squash-merge collapses authors, work content is correctly Vasquez's per the inbox memo. Worth a brief PR-body note for reviewer context.

**Procedural — Wave 5 cross-lane bundling + W2-W5 trend (documented in decisions.md as a dedicated subsection):**
- **Apone's commit `b346157`** ("ci(slsa): unify SLSA provenance + SBOM under multi-subject in-toto predicate") landed git-authored as `Apone (DevOps) <apone@squad.mahjong>` (author hygiene preamble held) but absorbed all of Hicks's frontend implementation work: `src/three-renderer.ts` (NEW, 78 lines), `src/scene-shell.ts` (rewritten, 106 changed), `src/voice.ts` (126 changed), `src/tournaments.ts` (289 changed), `src/game-bootstrap.ts` (27 changed), `scripts/generate-sw-manifest.js`, `tests/selectors.md` W5 footer (131 added), plus 11 built artefacts under `src/frontend/autotable/` (new `scene-shell` + `three-renderer` x2 + `tournaments` + `voice` + `game-bootstrap` + `toast` hashes; pruned the stale W4 hashes; updated `manifest-precache.json` + `index.html`).
- **Root cause:** during Apone's commit-tree recovery from a concurrent `.git/config` race (the `user.{name,email}` between `git config` SET and `git commit` was rewritten by a neighbouring agent run between the two steps), Apone's `git commit-tree` recovery ran against a working tree that already had Hicks's untracked frontend files staged via an earlier `git add`. The recovery commit absorbed them. Hicks then re-bundled differently — committing only the memo + history — once the working tree was unwedged.
- **W2-W3-W4-W5 trend.** Cross-lane bundling has now occurred in EVERY Phase K wave: W2 Bishop's commits absorbed Vasquez + Apone WIP; W3 Bishop's six backend commits git-authored as Vasquez (identity-clobber); W4 Bishop's `2265de8` swept all 7 Vasquez backend test files + the regression rename (content bundling); W5 Apone's `b346157` swept all of Hicks's frontend implementation (content bundling, OPPOSITE lane direction from W4). The W4 author-hygiene preamble fixes IDENTITY at commit-time but NOT cross-lane CONTENT bundling. The W5 Vasquez stash-checkpoint discipline fixes own-work PRESERVATION but does not stop a concurrent agent's `git add` from absorbing your untracked files.
- **Production impact:** ZERO. Squash-merge collapses per-commit authors; the PR-level `Co-authored-by: Copilot` trailer is the canonical attribution surface, and the trailer is preserved on `b346157` and `8b3051f`. The work content is correctly each agent's per the inbox memos + histories. Functionally complete.
- **Wave 6 remediation (mandatory — TWO new disciplines added to W6 prompt template):** (1) **per-invocation `git -c user.name=X -c user.email=Y commit ...`** instead of `git config user.name X` + later `git commit` — the `-c` form is race-safe because identity is bound to the exact `commit` invocation and cannot drift between SET and COMMIT; this RETIRES the W4 start-of-prompt `git config user.name` step. (2) **`flock /tmp/squad-git-lock git add … && git commit …` coordinator-side mutex** so agents serialise the git-write critical section (≤30s typical) — concurrent agents' `git add` cannot absorb your untracked files between your `git add` and your `git commit`. Both stack with the W4 identity preamble + W5 stash-checkpoint discipline.

**Notable observations:**
- **Scene-shell <500 kB Wave-2 target FINALLY closed in Wave 5** after three waves of deferral. The lazy `three-renderer` peel pattern (microscopic ~80-line coordinator + dynamic-imported renderer graph) hits **2.33 kB** vs the W4 monolithic 886.4 kB — 99.5% headroom under the 500 kB budget. Net renderer transfer on cold game URL is roughly unchanged (~872 kB across the lazy chunks) but the user experience flips: lobby paints in ~219 kB eager + 170 kB bootstrap, the renderer streams in parallel with tile-texture round-trips, and SW pre-cache (newly expanded to include `scene-shell` + both `three-renderer` sub-chunks) means warm returning users get WebGL in ~50 ms instead of ~3 s.
- **Author hygiene preamble (W4) STILL working at git-author level.** 11/11 W5 commits with correct git authors (Apone 6, Bishop 2, Hicks 1, Vasquez 1, Scribe 1 incoming). The remaining failure mode is cross-lane CONTENT bundling — addressed in W6 by the race-safe `git -c user.* commit` pattern + `flock` mutex.
- **Stash-checkpoint discipline shipped in `docs/agent-handoff-protocol.md`.** Vasquez formalised the W4 carryover into a canonical document — recommended adoption in W6 prompts. Pairs with (does NOT replace) the W4 author-hygiene preamble and the (incoming) W6 race-safe identity binding + `flock` mutex.
- **Hudson hand-off actioned — `RegressionHostFixture` ships + `MaxParallelThreads=2` workaround RETIRED.** Hudson did not action the CollectionFixture in W5 (other priorities, captured in `docs/test-harness-handoff.md` § "Phase K Wave 5 — addendum"); Vasquez implemented as part of the bring-up. `[CollectionDefinition("regression-host")]` exposing a shared `WebApplicationFactory<Program>` eliminates the W4 disposal race (`ObjectDisposedException` on shared sqlite connection when two collections raced through teardown — factory lifetime now scoped to the collection rather than the class). Closing W5 invocation is the plain `dotnet test src/backend/Mahjong.Autotable.slnx --nologo` — default xUnit parallelism, no workaround flag.
- **JWKS endpoint reservation pattern locked.** Any future cache-bypass slot (a `/.well-known/...` resource that doesn't exist yet but will) should ship the route NOW with `Cache-Control: no-store` so no CDN can pin a long-TTL parent-level 404 against it. Phase L flips the JWKS payload from the W5 stub `{ error, algorithm: "HS256", note }` to a real `{ keys: [...] }` RS256 array — the route already exists, only the controller body changes.
- **Wave-N+1 mirroring rule formalised by Apone.** Any prod-only data plane shipped in wave N (e.g. W4 `mahjong-jwt-keys`) MUST mirror into staging in wave N+1 (W5 `mahjong-jwt-keys-staging`). Otherwise the smoke test exercises the singular-key fallback path instead of the array-binding path it's supposed to gate. Forward-applicable to any future prod-data-plane shipment.
- **JWT singular `JwtSigningKey` fallback kept one more wave.** The W3 migration table said W5 would drop the legacy singular — reality is that the W4 `JwtSigningKeyProvider_FallsBackToLegacySingular` test still asserts the legacy path, so dropping the property would break the test. W6 drops it once Apone's SSM rotation drill exercises the array path in production. `docs/jwt-rotation.md` §7 reflects lived reality.
- **Two SLSA generator generations now coexist in Rekor.** W4 attestations (single-subject, container generator) remain forever-verifiable with the W4 `slsa-verifier verify-image` invocation. W5+ attestations use the generic generator (multi-subject) + the W5 `verify-artifact` shape. `docs/slsa-provenance.md` §6 carries the W4→W5 migration narrative; the lock-step invariant for renaming `sign-image.yml` / `slsa-provenance.yml` grew from 5 to 6 files (the new `attestations:` block in `kyverno-cosign-verify.yaml`).
- **4/4 author hygiene this wave** (Apone, Bishop, Hicks, Vasquez all configured cleanly). W4 prompt-template preamble carried the remediation forward + held this wave. The cross-lane content bundling remains the open failure mode — W6 mitigation locked in.

**Procedural — Wave 6 identity-race hardening landed and HELD (W5 cross-lane content bundling failure mode did NOT recur):**

- **Per-invocation `git -c user.name="<Agent>" -c user.email="<email>" commit ...` race-safe identity binding worked at the git-author level for all 5 W6 commits** (Vasquez `6630c6d`, Bishop `ef719df`, Apone `4fb22b6`, Hicks `191bf96`, Squad `abf7624`). `git log --format='%an <%ae>'` on each commit returns the intended agent identity verbatim. The W4→W5 incurable race (`git config` SET ↔ later `git commit` raced by a sibling agent's `git config` SET) is bypassed entirely because `-c` binds identity to the exact `commit` invocation rather than to persisted `.git/config` state.

- **Hicks's pre-flight `git config user.{name,email}` race-state was still observed in `.git/config` mid-wave** (a sibling agent's `git config` rewrote `[user]` between Hicks's `git config` SET and Hicks's later `git commit`). The per-invocation `-c` override BYPASSED the race entirely — the resulting `Author:` on Hicks's `191bf96` is `Hicks (Frontend) <hicks@squad.mahjong>` as intended. **This is the W6 win** — the race is incurable from the agent side, but the per-invocation pattern dodges it.

- **`flock -w 120 9 ... 9>/tmp/squad-git-lock` mutex** stacked with the per-invocation binding serialised the git-write critical section. 120s wait is empirically generous (typical commit + push ≤ 30s). No race observed during W6.

- **Lane-discipline CI shipped this wave + its first findings (2 legitimate cross-lane EDITS, NOT bundling).** `tests/ci/check-cross-lane-bundling.sh --pr HEAD --base origin/main` flagged Bishop's `ef719df` (touched `Phase_K_W3/GameVoiceEnabledFlagTests.cs` — Bishop legitimately patched a W3 test for the W6 voice-hub multi-hub surface) and Hicks's `191bf96` (touched `tests/selectors.md` — Hicks legitimately appended W6 testids to the shared selectors contract doc). Both are legitimate cross-lane EDITS rather than WIP-absorption bundling (work content correctly belongs to the editing agent per the inbox memos). Vasquez refined the script's `Phase_K_W*/<AgentName>/` attribution to avoid counting Bishop's own `Phase_K_W6/Bishop/BracketGeneratorDeterminismTests.cs` against him (without the refinement, **3** false positives). **W7 path-forward:** each agent opens their OWN PR + lane-discipline runs on each (formal single-lane PR enforcement). The W6 wave-level bring-up branch is the LAST wave-level squash-merge; W7+ stops the historical pattern.

- **Selective `git add <path>` only — NEVER `git add -A` / `git add .`** during cross-agent waves. The W5 cross-lane content bundling failure mode (Apone's `b346157` absorbing Hicks's frontend implementation; Bishop's `2265de8` absorbing Vasquez's tests in W4; Bishop's commits authored as Vasquez identity-clobber in W3) **dropped to zero in W6** with this discipline + the lane-discipline CI catching residual touches. The 2 lane-discipline-flagged cross-lane EDITS this wave are legitimate surface-shared touches, NOT WIP-absorption bundles.

**Notable observations:**

- **Identity-race hardening WIN.** The per-invocation `git -c user.name=X -c user.email=Y commit ...` pattern that landed in the W6 prompt template HELD across all 5 W6 commits despite the race-state being observed mid-wave. The W4 author-hygiene preamble approach (`git config user.name` at start of session) is officially RETIRED in favour of the per-invocation form; the W5 `flock` mutex stays in force as defence-in-depth.

- **Lane-discipline CI shipped + caught 2 legitimate cross-lane EDITS on first run.** The script's `Phase_K_W*/<AgentName>/` attribution refinement is a stable pattern that lets agents own subfolders within `Phase_K_W*/<AgentName>/` without counting against the cross-lane budget. **The historical wave-level bring-up branch pattern stops at W6** — W7 enforces single-lane PRs.

RS256 JWT defaults `HS256`; livestream HLS controller returns 404 until recording starts; SpectatorVoiceHub returns `sfu://stub/{tableId}`; commentary generator returns the canonical "not yet available" stub; double-elim persists only winners-bracket round 1 today. **Phase L flips each surface from stub → real implementation without changing the controller URL or audit Kind.**

- **JWKS algorithm-branch contract.** The W5 reserved-slot pattern (`Cache-Control: no-store` 404 with `{ error, algorithm: "HS256" }` body) flipped this wave to the W6 contract: HS256 returns 404 + `max-age=60` (short TTL so a downstream CDN can't pin the 404 forever and block the eventual RS256 flip) + structured `{ reason, migrateTo, migrate_to }` body; RS256 returns 200 + real JWKS body + `max-age=3600`. The Wave-5 `JwksEndpointContractTests` was updated to the W6 contract by Bishop in his single commit `ef719df`.

**Procedural — Wave 7 identity-race hardening + strict-mode lane-discipline both held across the second consecutive wave (the W3/W4/W5 cross-lane content bundling failure mode remains broken):**

- **Per-invocation `git -c user.name="<Agent>" -c user.email="<email>" commit ...` race-safe identity binding worked at the git-author level for all 19 W7 commits** (Bishop's 7: `08aa67c` `2be96a9` `0ecc238` `7542234` `1032243` `a98a132` `64fbee8`; Hicks's 2: `2a7f8a7` `6aa8a18`; Vasquez's 4: `3c37ee8` `15508fc` `a4482b2` `02b0fa6`; Apone's 6: `1beff2f` `5a5d5f9` `4c6d9b6` `a566992` `34b0a0c` `996f209`). `git log --format='%an <%ae>'` on each commit returns the intended agent identity verbatim. The W4→W5 incurable race (`git config` SET ↔ later `git commit` raced by a sibling agent's `git config` SET) is bypassed entirely because `-c` binds identity to the exact `commit` invocation rather than to persisted `.git/config` state. **W6 introduction + W7 second-wave standing = pattern is durable.**

- **`flock -w 120 9 ... 9>/tmp/squad-git-lock` mutex** stacked with the per-invocation binding serialised the git-write critical section. 120s wait is empirically generous (typical commit + push ≤ 30s). No race observed during W7.

- **Lane-discipline strict mode shipped this wave + its first findings (2 legitimate cross-lane EDITS, NOT bundling).** `tests/ci/check-cross-lane-bundling.sh --strict` with `STRICT=1` flagged Bishop's `1032243` (touched `tests/Shims/CommentaryGeneratorTestShim.cs` — Vasquez file; Bishop legitimately added the additive `GenerateRecords()` method needed for his W7 `CommentaryRecord` contract tests per the explicit W7-brief delegation note) and Hicks's `2a7f8a7` (touched `src/frontend/autotable-src/tests/selectors.md` — Vasquez-lane path per current map; Hicks legitimately appended W7 testids to the shared selectors contract doc, the W6 pattern that already exists). Both are legitimate cross-lane EDITS rather than WIP-absorption bundling (work content correctly belongs to the editing agent per the inbox memos). **W8 hand-off: refine `tests/ci/lane-map.json` to recognise `selectors.md` as a Hicks/Vasquez shared file** (the `selectors.md` finding is a true positive of legitimate shared-doc usage; the lane-map should encode it explicitly under `shared` paths OR add an explicit `selectors.md → hicks` override).

- **Selective `git add <path>` only — NEVER `git add -A` / `git add .`** during cross-agent waves. The W5 cross-lane content bundling failure mode (Apone's `b346157` absorbing Hicks's frontend implementation in W5; Bishop's `2265de8` absorbing Vasquez's tests in W4; Bishop's commits authored as Vasquez identity-clobber in W3) **dropped to zero in W6 and stayed at zero in W7** with this discipline + the lane-discipline CI catching residual touches. The 2 lane-discipline-flagged cross-lane EDITS this wave are legitimate surface-shared touches, NOT WIP-absorption bundles.

- **No Coordinator fix-up commit required this wave.** W6 needed `abf7624` to land the 7-line append to `infra/k8s/base/kustomization.yaml`'s `resources:` block. W7 closes 1506/0/0 with no Coordinator intervention; the agent commits compose end-to-end.

**Notable observations:**

- **Identity-race hardening + lane-discipline strict-mode WIN at the second consecutive wave.** The per-invocation `git -c user.name=X -c user.email=Y commit ...` pattern that landed in the W6 prompt template HELD across all 19 W7 commits. The `flock` mutex held. The strict-mode CI caught only legitimate cross-lane edits (no WIP bundles). **The W3/W4/W5 cross-lane content bundling trend stays broken at W7.** Pattern is now production-grade across 25+ commits over 2 waves.

- **Vite bundler swap landed.** The W5→W6→W7 deferred bundler decision finally closes. Renderer payload total down 22.7 % (838.82 → 648.07 kB). Three-renderer big chunk down 21.8 % (739.72 → 578.72 kB). **Soft pass against the strict <550 KB target** (578.72 kB is slightly above; W8 hand-off via `three/src/*` deep imports). Decision matrix locked in `docs/frontend-build-tooling.md`.

- **CSP narrowed to `script-src 'self'`.** Vendored HLS.js via `import('hls.js/dist/hls.light.mjs')` retires the W6 `https://cdn.jsdelivr.net` allowance. Real supply-chain trust-boundary win — every external `script-src` URL is a separate trust boundary that can be compromised; we retired one.

- **Six-file signer-identity invariant is machine-enforced.** Apone's `scripts/check_signer_identity.py` + `.pre-commit-config.yaml` hook with `always_run: true, pass_filenames: false` (drift is a CROSS-FILE property; staged-file scoping would miss drift) replaces the W6 manual lock-step. The W7 spec-path `infra/k8s/policies/kyverno-enforce-patch.yaml` was reconciled to the actual `infra/k8s/overlays/prod/kyverno-enforce-patch.yaml` location; W8 audits the path-reconciliation choice.

- **Helm chart-of-charts shipped at parity with Kustomize, NOT as a migration.** The W5→W6→W7 deferred helm chart-of-charts work landed. `helm/mahjong/` umbrella + 3 subcharts (`mahjong-api`, `mahjong-coturn`, `mahjong-postgres-sidecar`) with `alias:` on every dependency (non-optional — Helm routes umbrella values by chart NAME without it). CI deploy stays on Kustomize; Helm is the operator-driven point-install + partner-deploy surface. W7 acceptance gate is parity, both paths render equivalent objects. W9 re-evaluates migration.

- **21-wave zero-skip streak.** Phase K Wave 7 is the 21st consecutive green wave (J.1 → J.10 + K.1 → K.7). The `MaxParallelThreads=2` workaround stays retired courtesy of Vasquez's W5 `RegressionHostFixture` `[CollectionDefinition]`. Default xUnit parallelism runs green over consecutive gate invocations.

- **Real-implementation flips landed for 3 W6 forward-staged surfaces.** RS256 JWT flipped from config-toggle stub to e2e issuer-claim + rotation drill + algorithm-confusion guard. Voice livestream HLS flipped from in-memory stub to real ffmpeg subprocess + boot probe. Phase L commentary moved from envelope stub to canonical `CommentaryRecord[]` records contract. **Phase L is increasingly back-loaded with real LLM commentary + Janus SFU integration as the only remaining stub-to-real flips.**

- **OIDC discovery `issuer` resolution pattern** is the new auth surface contract: `ConfiguredIssuer ?? ${scheme}://${host}`. Empty `AuthOptions.Issuer` means "fall back to request origin"; the `iss` claim is only stamped when non-empty so HS256 baseline tokens stay shape-compatible with the W4 verifier. Operators can ship RS256 to staging without `Auth:Issuer` and override cleanly for production behind a load balancer where the `Host` header doesn't reflect the public hostname.

- **Three-renderer big-chunk <540 KB strict target MET at 531.86 KB.** Two-wave cumulative reduction across W6 → W8: −27.9 % (739.72 → 531.86 kB). The W7 hand-off hint about `three/src/*` deep imports was tested empirically and FOUND WRONG for three.js 0.179 — both per-class deep imports and bulk `from 'three/src/Three.js'` made the bundle ~150 KB LARGER. Root cause: three's bundled `build/three.module.js` is more tree-shake-friendly than its `src/` tree because `moduleSideEffects: false` Rollup config can dead-strip private helpers inside a single bundled file but conservatively preserves them across file boundaries. Reference rewriters kept in-tree but MUST NOT be applied to source by default. Documented in `docs/frontend-three-budget.md §4`. The W8 levers that actually worked were the GLTFLoader chunk peel (single-line `manualChunks` pre-check addition; −44.22 KB) + the hand-rolled `mergeSimpleGeometries` helper (36-line drop-in for `BufferGeometryUtils.mergeGeometries`, contract-restricted to non-indexed inputs with shared attribute layout; −3.83 KB).

This closes the W7 hand-off item #2 (Apone) that was carried over from the original W6 six-file signer-identity invariant scope. Combined with the new `PATH_CONFUSION_GUARDS` tuple in `scripts/check_signer_identity.py` (presence-check tuple `(canonical, wrong, reason)` evaluated alongside the regex-check), the W7 wrong-path `infra/k8s/policies/kyverno-enforce-patch.yaml` mode-of-failure is now closed.

Driven by 6 durable-backing flips for W8 in-memory surfaces: EF commentary usage meter (`(PeriodYear, PeriodMonth)` unique index; `IsConcurrencyToken()` NOT `IsRowVersion()` because SQLite has no native rowversion — manual `RowVersion = Guid.NewGuid().ToByteArray()` bump preserves the optimistic-concurrency contract identical across all three providers; HTTP 429 `{ error: "monthly-token-cap" }` envelope when `ThrowOnMonthlyCap` is true), `EfIdempotencyStore` with `DefaultReplayWindow = 5min` Stripe-convention + defensive `ExpiresAt <= now` check on read so correctness doesn't depend on the sweeper running, Janus readiness supervisor with cold-start optimisation (Unknown → Bound flips on first healthy probe to skip the 30s warmup), `RotationCadenceValidator` aborting boot on `JwksCacheTtlSeconds > RotationGracePeriodSeconds / 2` (Nyquist margin: downstream verifiers refresh at least twice during the grace window), `SignalRBackpressureBroadcaster<THub>` generic with `DefaultMaxMessagesPerSecond = 30` SignalR-canonical ceiling + `DefaultMaxMessageAgeSeconds = 5` replay-drop window + `DefaultRetainedMessageCount = 256` LinkedList ring buffer per group, and the livestream 301/308 alias controller (W9 identity decision `tableId ≡ gameId`). **The remaining stub-to-real flips are getting shorter: real commentary + Janus landed at W8; durable backings + readiness gating + idempotency cross-replica + rotation cadence + uniform hub shape are LANDED at W9; the W10 ask is the StackExchange.Redis client wire + the hub retrofit + the sweeper hosted service.**

- **Three-renderer big-chunk <510 KB strict target MET at 507.47 KB.** Four-wave cumulative reduction across W6 → W9: **−31.5 %** (739.72 → 507.47 kB). The W8 §4 empirical rejection of deep-imports (the W8 directive's hint about `three/src/*` deep imports tested + EMPIRICALLY REJECTED at +150 KB larger because three's bundled `build/three.module.js` is more tree-shake-friendly than its `src/` tree under the `moduleSideEffects: false` Rollup config) holds at W9 — the W9 levers were two `enforce: 'pre'` Vite transform plugins (`stripUnusedThreeMaterials` + `stripModuleFeatures`) that statically transform the imported three.js source. Smoke-tested via headless Playwright (0 JS errors, canvas renders). Full autopsy in `docs/frontend-three-budget.md §5`.

- **PWA-Builder is the W9+ PWA installability audit tool.** `lighthouse@13.3.0` is the new permanent devDep pin; the PWA category is gone from LH13 (only `viewport` survives under `best-practices`). Recipe documented in `docs/frontend-pwa-audit.md §3`: build → serve → LH13 categories → PWA Builder manual report card → manifest-lint substitute. CI/CLI wiring of PWA Builder deferred to W10 pending a public preview URL. **The W8 hand-off about Lighthouse 13+ migration is now CLOSED.**

- **Canonical bracket wire-shape adoption is HARD.** `DoubleElimRenderer.render` rejects unknown shapes with a visible `<div data-testid="bracket-shape-error" role="alert">` div + console.error citing `docs/contracts/bracket-api.md`. The W6 `partitionDoubleElim` heuristic still compiles for unit tests but production code no longer reaches it. **Migration discipline pinned:** Bishop flag-gates dual fields for one wave → Hicks normalises → Vasquez updates mocks → Bishop drops flag. **The W8 hand-off about canonical `DoubleElimLayout` wire-spelling is now CLOSED via the bracket-shape-error hard gate.**

- **Three independent canary gates, not one composite.** Argo Rollouts evaluates `successRate.threshold: 0.99` + `p99Latency.threshold: 500ms` + `errorBudget.threshold: 14.4` (Google SRE Workbook canonical fast-burn against `sloErrorRate: 0.01`) in parallel; ANY single failure aborts. **No aggregation logic in the chart** — a composite obscures WHICH dimension broke, and three independent gates also let an operator disable one (e.g. no latency histogram instrumentation yet) without removing the gate entirely.

- **Mobile hotfix uses a SEPARATE 2-reviewer environment.** `release-channel-production-hotfix` is distinct from the routine `release-channel-production`. **The decision gate (to skip External-Testing) is what needs the second pair of eyes, not the output gate (build+submit).** Three durable audit-trail markers per run (warning log, step-summary banner, Slack notification). Default rollout 100% Android — a hotfix worth skipping soak is worth fully replacing the broken build immediately.

- **`scripts/check_invariants.py` is the new extension point for cross-file invariants.** Generalises the W7 signer-identity guard pattern via a single `INVARIANTS` tuple + append-only extension point; **wraps the W7 `check_signer_identity.py` via subprocess (NOT import)** so a Python exception in one doesn't pollute the other's traceback and each script remains independently runnable. W9 ships one binding: `JwtRsaKeys` lock-step across 7 surfaces (prod + staging ESO manifests + 4 helm values files + `docs/jwt-rotation.md`).

- **`TileReference(string TileId, string Suit, int Rank)` is the canonical commentary tile-ref shape across backend + frontend.** Bishop's record + `Parse` factory + `Unknown` sentinel on the backend; Hicks consumes `{tileId, suit, rank}` shape on the frontend with W9-string fallback retained for one wave (planned removal **W12 after Bishop ships two consecutive backend deploys on the object shape**). Property name `TileReferences` preserved across the shape change (Vasquez W9 regression invariant). Canonical contract pinned in `docs/contracts/commentary-tile-ref.md` (NEW). Hicks's commentary chip clicks dispatch `mahjong:highlight-tile` on `document` with `{ tileId, source: 'commentary-panel' }` — the `source` discriminator lets the W9 3D mesh pulse listener distinguish chip-click invocation from other call sites.

- **Janus surface is now 3-level + reaped, not binary + leaking.** W9 shipped binary `Healthy`/`Unhealthy` + on-demand mountpoint creation; W10 adds the `Degraded` mid-state for amber-alerting before the trip and the `JanusMountpointLifecycleService` `BackgroundService` (60s sweep, 5min idle TTL, internal `RunOnce` for deterministic tests). Long-running deployments no longer accumulate orphan mountpoints from disconnected spectator sessions.

- **JWT rotation cadence flipped from 180d → 90d (quarterly).** Squad-wide secret-management cadence review settled on 90d for all secrets (JWT signing keys, OAuth client secrets, Redis auth-tokens, DB passwords). **Cognitive load per rotation is LOWER under the new walk-through even though cadence doubled** because §3.1 is the quarterly calendar, §3.2 is the full `aws ssm put-parameter` walkthrough with pre-/post-flight JWKS validation, §3.3 is the hand-off checklist, §3.4 is the rollback procedure (promote previous → active + archive → previous — the just-minted key is **intentionally discarded** because a key clients have rejected is a key we never want to revisit). **First quarterly rotation under the new cadence: end of September 2026 (Q3 2026)** — W11 on-call SRE inherits.

- **`prod-health-check.yml` synthetic edge probe is a BACKSTOP, not a pager replacement.** Sentry + Prometheus fire FASTER for app-side issues; the synthetic probe catches cases the reactive stack misses (JWKS publication breakage when no client has yet tried the affected `kid`; metrics endpoint returning 200 with empty body after a partial restart) and gives the on-call a written record when the reactive stack is itself degraded. **3-strike open / 2-strike close cooldown** with state machine carried in a hidden HTML comment (`<!-- prod-health-check:state strikes=N recoveries=M -->`) embedded in the incident issue body — machine-parseable + survives manual triage edits + invisible in rendered issue.

- **`container-scan-remediation.yml` opens issues, NOT PRs.** Alpine v3.19 → v3.20 occasionally ships breaking-glibc changes; the squad reviews CVE remediation before bumping. Suggested base-image bump in the issue body is a HINT, not an auto-PR. De-duplicates against existing open issues by title prefix `[container-scan] CVE remediation` + labels `security,automated`.

- **`maxmemory-policy=allkeys-lru` is canonical for the idempotency cache.** Bishop's store is a CACHE, not a primary. `volatile-lru` would silently drop idempotency keys with no TTL. Apone's Redis Terraform module pins `allkeys-lru` via custom `aws_elasticache_parameter_group`.

- **CHANGELOG version-arithmetic check goes in every changelog-bump pattern.** W10 task prompt said "CHANGELOG bump to 0.18.0" but that version was already used by W9; project versions track the wave count (W1+W2=0.11.0, W3=0.12.0, …, W9=0.18.0, **W10=0.19.0**). Document the previous version + the new version explicitly in every wave-decision memo to catch the off-by-one before commit.

- **`[Collection("DbSerial")]` is the W9-retro action item closed at W10.** Vasquez ships the collection definition + the policy doc; Bishop's W11 deliverable opts the SQLite-heavy contract test classes (`Phase_K_W9/Bishop/IdempotencyStoreContractTests.cs`, `Phase_K_W10/Bishop/RedisIdempotencyStoreLiveTests.cs`) into the collection. The collection definition also de-flaked an intermittent Bishop W9 fact (`RedisWrapper_ExposesConnectionString`) that occasionally failed under parallel execution.

- **`docs/test-architecture.md` + `docs/agent-handoff-protocol.md §5` are the new canonical consolidations.** Vasquez's W10 docs cover the parallelism policy (`MaxParallelThreads=2` retired W5; `RegressionHostFixture` collection; new `DbSerial` collection) + coverage pyramid (W10 baseline + W11+ gap analysis: tournament bracket E2E happy path, Janus negative-path contract facts, Dutch-Swiss algorithmic unit facts, prod-env helm release manifest parity contract tests) + concurrent-agent test safety guarantees (§5.1 lock critical section, §5.2 backup discipline, §5.3 stash-discipline NEVER `--include-untracked`, §5.4 `shared_files` allowlist, §5.5 rebase-inside-flock, §5.6 `[Collection("DbSerial")]` policy, §5.7 branch-protection alignment, §5.8 pre-commit checklist).

The `MaxParallelThreads=2` workaround stays retired courtesy of Vasquez's W5 `RegressionHostFixture` `[CollectionDefinition]` + the new W10 `DbSerial` collection for SQLite-heavy migrations.

The barrel re-export tables stay intact — only the GLSL body is emptied. Safe per scene-graph audit confirming only `MeshBasicMaterial` + `MeshLambertMaterial` + `LineBasicMaterial` + W7 `CustomOutline` ShaderMaterial are constructed in `autotable-src`. **−31.04 KB in one wave** — largest single-wave bundle delta since W7 Vite swap.

Argo Rollouts dashboard sits behind the existing prod oauth2-proxy + dex OIDC chain — Apone+Vasquez decision to reuse rather than introduce a new identity provider. JWT rehearsal is **staging-only** with a hard step-1 gate `target_env != staging → exit 1` (no prod opt-in) so the on-call SRE can practice the EXACT sequence the week before the real Q3 2026 prod rotation. `prod-health-check.yml` rewritten single-region → 4-region matrix (`us-east-1` / `us-west-2` / `eu-west-1` / `ap-southeast-1`) with aggregator job + per-region HTML state markers; opens on ANY region's `strikes ≥ 3`, closes only when ALL four regions show `recoveries ≥ 2`. `docs/edge-region-probes.md` ships the **per-pattern failure-mode playbook** (1-region / 2-region / 4-region patterns each get a first-look + an action path).

- **Lane-discipline strict-mode `checked=4 violations=0` — the FIRST 0-VIOLATION LANE-DISCIPLINE WAVE.** Vasquez's lane-map broadenings closed both W10 hand-offs: `shims_shared` (4-author, primary `vasquez`, covers `Shims/*` Phase J compatibility shim layer) collapses the recurring Bishop additive-shim pattern; `pwa_audit_workflow_shared` (2-author, primary `apone` + co-author `hicks`, covers `pwa-audit*.yml` + `pwa-builder*.yml`) closes the Hicks workflow attribution pattern. `tests/ci/check-cross-lane-bundling.sh` `is_shared_file()` + `shared_file_authors()` extended to recognise both new entries. **SIXTH CONSECUTIVE WAVE WITH ZERO IDENTITY DRIFT + ZERO COORDINATOR FIX-UP.**

**Notable observations:**

- **Identity-race hardening WIN at the SIXTH consecutive wave + lock-file cutover holds at 2nd consecutive fully-adopted wave.** The per-invocation `git -c user.name=X -c user.email=Y commit ...` pattern that landed in the W6 prompt template HELD across all 4 W11 rollup commits. The `flock -w 120 9 ... 9>.work/squad-git-lock` mutex held — and the W10 formal cutover is now operating in its second wave with no regressions. **Pattern is now production-grade across 60+ commits over 6 waves.**

- **+295 net passing in one wave — NEW LARGEST single-wave delta of Phase K so far** (W10 was +228, W8 +200, W9 +174). Driven by 6 backend surface deliveries: FIDE C.04 backtracking Swiss with `ISwissTiebreakStrategy` plug-in interface (replacing W10 single-swap; default tiebreak stack is `Buchholz → Berger → rating-asc`; DutchSwissPairingService now functionally subsumed and W12 retirement candidate), TileReference 4-byte binary codec (`TileReferencesBinary` additive field on `CommentaryRecord` alongside W10 JSON field; both ship — consumers pick by capability negotiation; `FromBinary` returns `Unknown` on checksum mismatch — never throws), mountpoint-eviction SignalR metric tie-in (`reason="mountpoint_evicted"` joins W10 `rate_cap`/`send_failure`/`age_window` taxonomy), age-at-publish histogram (canonical p99 envelope-age surface for the SLO dashboard), EfCommentaryStore persistence (EF migrations × 3 providers Sqlite/Postgres/SqlServer; daily retention sweep with `Func<DateTimeOffset>` clock injection for tests; `DefaultRetentionDays = 7` pinned by test), RFC 7662 OAuth introspection endpoint (`/oauth/introspect` with Basic-auth client credentials; §2.2 per-token-error 200 vs transport-error 4xx; `OAuthIntrospectionDisabledFacts` covers kill switch).

- **Three-renderer big-chunk <475 KB stretch target BEAT by 9 KB at 466.40 KB.** Six-wave cumulative reduction across W6 → W11: **−37.0 %** (739.72 → 466.40 kB). The ShaderChunk barrel surgery was the lever — new Vite plugin `stripUnusedShaderChunks()` with `enforce: 'pre'` empties GLSL bodies of 32 ShaderLib `vertex$X`/`fragment$X` constants + `cube_uv_reflection_fragment` ShaderChunk + standalone VSM-blur pair while keeping barrel re-export tables intact. The exclusions (`$a` meshbasic shared by `MeshBasicMaterial`+`LineBasicMaterial`; `$9` meshlambert) are documented from the scene-graph audit. The W12 hand-off candidates (`opaque_fragment`, `colorspace_fragment`, `tonemapping_*`, `UniformsLib` unused entries, `shadowmap_*` chunk bodies) collectively yield ~17-23 KB more headroom.

- **`?action=*` PWA shortcut deep-link routing wires BEFORE the W2 game-bootstrap guard.** Critical invariant: the heavy renderer chunk must not be imported when a shortcut URL is opened. Supported keywords are `new-game` / `spectate` / `tournament` (+ `tournaments` plural alias) — the W10→W11 `?action=tournaments` → `?action=tournament` flip is preserved as a back-compat alias for installed PWAs that cached the W10 manifest. Full contract in `docs/frontend-routing.md` (NEW W11).

- **Vasquez's W11 lane-map broadening achieved the 0-violation stretch goal.** `shims_shared` (4-author) + `pwa_audit_workflow_shared` (2-author) entries close all known false-positive bundling patterns from W7 + W10. The strict-mode gate now fires only on actual cross-lane regressions. **`docs/agent-handoff-protocol.md §5.9` shared-files registry policy** is the canonical doc for adding future `*_shared` entries (problem statement → entry shape → notify `.squad/decisions/inbox/`).

- **Apone+Vasquez decision: reuse existing prod oauth2-proxy + dex OIDC chain for Argo Rollouts dashboard** rather than introduce a new identity provider. The chain already covers @squad.mahjong + allow-listed external observers per `docs/oauth-production-setup.md §4`. **NetworkPolicy deferred to W12** — the auth-aware ingress is the auth boundary at the cluster edge but does NOT prevent in-cluster bypass (a pod in another namespace could hit the Service directly). NetworkPolicy denying ingress from outside the `argo-rollouts` namespace closes that gap.

- **Range-floor + exact-pin Terraform CLI version policy.** Codified at `docs/terraform.md §6`. Quarterly cadence anchored on wave bring-up: W8=1.9.8 → W11=1.10.5 → W14=TBD likely 1.11.x. Range floor in modules stays at `>= 1.5.0` (forward-compatible with operators running 1.11/1.12/1.15 locally — they aren't blocked). Exact pin in CI workflows. Out-of-band on CVE. Why NOT 1.15.x: at W11 cut, 1.10 is the current STABLE minor; 1.11+ are unreleased or fresh-cut.

- **JWT rotation rehearsal harness is staging-only with hard step-1 gate `target_env != staging → exit 1`.** Prod rotation is operator-manual on purpose; runbook owner is the on-call SRE, not the workflow. The rehearsal builds muscle memory — it does NOT replace the runbook. The hard gate prevents an accidental dispatch from rotating prod keys in a window the on-call SRE didn't schedule. **Canonical pattern for future recurring operator drills:** ship a matching rehearsal harness BEFORE the first real execution.

- **Multi-region prod-health-check matrix needs a fan-out failure-mode playbook.** `docs/edge-region-probes.md §5` is the canonical playbook. On-call SRE's response is sharply different between "1/4 tripped" (regional CDN problem) and "4/4 tripped" (global outage) — the per-pattern playbook is the runbook's value-add.

- **CHANGELOG version-arithmetic check held at W11.** W10 retro called out the 0.18.0 typo in Stephen's W10 prompt; W11 sidesteps the same risk by reading the W10 `CHANGELOG.md [0.19.0]` entry directly and bumping to `0.20.0`. Wave-count-tracks-version is the invariant.

- **`.NET` DI does NOT inject optional ctor params with default-null values.** Use an explicit factory delegate with `sp.GetService<T>()` (returns null gracefully). Pattern lives at `Program.cs` for both `JwksCacheService` (W10) and `JanusMountpointLifecycleService` (W10/W11).

- **26-wave zero-skip streak.** Phase K Wave 11 is the 26th consecutive green wave (J.1 → J.10 + K.1 → K.11). The `MaxParallelThreads=2` workaround stays retired courtesy of Vasquez's W5 `RegressionHostFixture` `[CollectionDefinition]` + the W10 `DbSerial` collection definition (Bishop's `[Collection("DbSerial")]` tagging of EF-touching test classes is the W12 hand-off).

W12 wires it. URL strip-rewrite-on-success (`history.replaceState(null, '', '/replay/<id>')`) + toast-on-any-failure (the replay loader's existing `showErrorToast()` surface). **NO fallback to legacy `/api/games/{gameId}/replay`** — the W11 endpoint was a stopgap; W12 endpoint is the canonical surface per `docs/replay-storage.md §2`. If the new endpoint is unavailable (5xx) the toast surfaces an actionable error; users CANNOT silently roll back to the legacy endpoint because the canonical schema differs (the new endpoint returns `{ replayId, gameId, recordedAt, schemaVersion: 2, ... }` vs legacy `{ moves, players }`). Hicks's `src/frontend/autotable-src/src/replay-loader.ts` (NEW W12) is sole owner; full contract in `docs/frontend-routing.md §4.2` (NEW W12).

- **PMREMGenerator-adjacent ShaderChunk strip + `UniformsLib` unused-entry strip + `tonemapping_pars_fragment` + `colorspace_pars_fragment` strips collectively saved 17.75 KB.** Trajectory now `740 → 579 → 532 → 507 → 497 → 466 → 448 KB` across W6 → W7 → W8 → W9 → W10 → W11 → W12 (7-wave monotonic-decrease; cumulative −39.4%). The W11 ShaderChunk barrel surgery (`stripUnusedShaderChunks()` Vite plugin) is now augmented with: (a) six `envmap_*` entries from the PMREMGenerator-adjacent chunk family — safe because autotable's three-renderer doesn't construct PMREMGenerator or any `MeshStandardMaterial` / `MeshPhysicalMaterial` instances; (b) `tonemapping_pars_fragment` — safe because autotable doesn't use any of three.js's tonemapping operators (`LinearToneMapping` / `ReinhardToneMapping` / `CineonToneMapping` / `ACESFilmicToneMapping`); (c) `colorspace_pars_fragment` — autotable uses `LinearSRGBColorSpace` only and doesn't convert at the shader level. The 16 `UniformsLib` strips are uniforms-table entries the three-renderer doesn't reference (verified by `grep -r "UniformsLib\.\(points\|sprite\|...\)"` across `autotable-src/`). **W13 hand-off candidates (`shadowmap_*` chunk body strip ~6 KB + `points_vert` / `points_frag` ~3 KB + `sprite_vert` / `sprite_frag` ~3 KB + LineSegments unused vertex stage ~2 KB) collectively yield ~14 KB more headroom toward a <435 KB W13 stretch target.**

- **DutchSwissPairingService retirement closes the W11 hand-off.** The W11→W12 plan was: W11 ships FIDE C.04 behind `ISwissTiebreakStrategy` plug-in interface (Dutch + FIDE C.04 both live); W12 retires Dutch once `ISwissTiebreakStrategy` interface holds across all consumers.
