The W10 single-swap + float-down + `__bye__` insertion logic moves into `FideC04BacktrackingPairingService.cs` as a FALLBACK ONLY when the backtracking solver exceeds `floatAttempts < b.Count` cap — a defensive guard, not the primary path. Documented at `docs/tournament-architecture.md §4.3` (NEW W12).

- **`TileReference` reserved-byte usage carved out three flags + checksum.** The W11 4-byte codec reserved bytes 1-2 for "red-five / aka-dora flags + checksum"; W12 pins the exact bit allocation: byte 1 bit 0 = `HasRedFive`, byte 1 bit 1 = `IsAkaDora`, byte 1 bit 2 = `IsTsumogiri` (self-drawn discard marker — needed for commentary streaming); byte 1 bits 3-7 reserved (5 unused bits for future expansion). Byte 2 is reserved zero-padding. Byte 3 stays checksum (XOR of bytes 0-2). `FromBinary` returns `Unknown` on checksum mismatch — never throws. Documented at `docs/commentary-tile-codec.md §2` (NEW W12).

- **`CommentaryRecord` polymorphism via `[JsonDerivedType]` (System.Text.Json) discriminator on the `Kind` property.** Three subtypes ship in W12: `CommentaryRecord.RiichiCall`, `CommentaryRecord.Discard`, `CommentaryRecord.Pung`. STJ + Newtonsoft round-trip parity confirmed via `tests/Mahjong.Tests/Commentary/CommentaryRecordSerializationFacts.cs` (12 cases). The `[JsonDerivedType(typeof(RiichiCall), "riichi-call")]` discriminator value is the canonical wire surface for the W11 SignalR commentary feed. **`SystemTextJson` is the canonical serializer for `CommentaryRecord` polymorphism** — Newtonsoft round-trip parity is a regression guard, not a primary path.

- **Janus mountpoint admission control: `MaxConcurrentMountpoints=128` cap + ITU-T Y.1541 queue-by-priority.** The W10 → W11 trajectory added the mountpoint-eviction SignalR metric tie-in (`reason="mountpoint_evicted"`); W12 adds admission control at the OTHER end of the lifecycle. When a `Voice:JanusMountpointService.AcquireAsync()` call arrives and concurrent count is at cap, the queue-by-priority surface (`Premium` > `Standard` > `Spectator`) determines which awaits and which gets rejected with `mountpoint_admission_rejected_total{reason="capacity"}`. ITU-T Y.1541 priority tiers are the IPv4 DSCP-equivalent surface — documented at `docs/voice-sfu-design.md §4.6` (NEW W12).

- **Replay-by-id endpoint `/api/replays/{replayId}` is the canonical replay storage shape.** The legacy W2 `/api/games/{gameId}/replay` endpoint stays alive but is documented as DEPRECATED W12 in `docs/replay-storage.md §1`. The canonical schema is at `§2` with `schemaVersion: 2` marker; consumers MUST check `schemaVersion` and fall back to schema 1 only when reading legacy stored replays. Bishop's `IReplayStore` interface ships in `a3a8788` with `EfReplayStore` implementation (EF migrations × 3 providers Sqlite/Postgres/SqlServer). `ReplayStorageOptions.DefaultRetentionDays = 30` pinned by test (canonical replay retention — distinct from commentary retention `= 7`).

**Notable observations:**

- **Identity-race hardening WIN at the EIGHTH consecutive wave + lock-file cutover holds at 4th consecutive fully-adopted wave.** The per-invocation `git -c user.name=X -c user.email=Y commit ...` pattern that landed in the W6 prompt template HELD across all 5 W13 commits (4 agent rollups + 1 Vasquez same-lane amend). The `flock -w 120 9 ... 9>.work/squad-git-lock` mutex held — the W10 formal cutover is now operating in its fourth wave with no regressions. **Pattern is production-grade across 75+ commits over 8 waves.** Apone's `docs/agent-handoff-protocol.md §5.10` per-invocation identity binding template + `§6.1` flock cutover ledger remain canonical.

- **+179 net passing in one wave — 6th-largest single-wave delta of Phase K** (W11 +295, W10 +228, W12 +207, W8 +200, W9 +174, W7 +84). Mid-pack but seats a near-doubling of the W6 baseline (1422 → 2789 = +96.1 % cumulative). Driven primarily by Bishop's 7 backend surface deliveries flipping all 7 W12 forward-stage soft-pins to hard-asserts in one commit + Vasquez's 10 W13 contract tests + 12 W13 smokes + 6 Playwright specs.

- **Three-renderer big-chunk <440 KB stretch target BEAT by ~34 KB at 406.64 KB.** Eight-wave cumulative reduction across W6 → W13: **−45.0%** (739.72 → 406.64 kB). The deeper PMREMGenerator + UniformsLib strip was the lever — `SHADER_CHUNKS_TO_EMPTY` grew from 11 to 53 entries (+42 chunks across `bsdfs`/`lights_*`/`meshphysical_*`/`normal_*`/`roughnessmap_*`/`metalnessmap_*`/`clearcoat_*`/`iridescence_*`/`sheen_*`/`transmission_*`/`aomap_*`/`lightmap_*`/`emissivemap_*` families) + UniformsLib keys grew from 5 to 14 (+9 keys: `envmap`/`aomap`/`lightmap`/`emissivemap`/`bumpmap`/`normalmap`/`displacementmap`/`fog`/`lights`). The W14 hand-off candidates (`tonemapping_*` family + `encodings_pars_fragment` + `packing` + second-pass UniformsLib `points`/`sprite`/`linedashed`) collectively yield ~10-15 KB more headroom toward a <380 KB W14 stretch target.

- **`?action=spectate&gameId=<id>` PWA deep-link routing wires BEFORE the W2 game-bootstrap guard.** Critical invariant maintained from W11/W12: the heavy renderer chunk must not be imported when a shortcut URL is opened. `action-router.ts` is sole owner of dispatch; `openSpectatorLivestream` is called DIRECTLY (not via lazy-import — spectator chunk already bundled). **`history.replaceState` path+hash quirk** captured at `docs/frontend-action-router.md §6`: combined path+hash replaceState does NOT emit `hashchange`; direct call avoids no-hashchange gotcha. **NO fallback to legacy unsigned `?gameId=<id>` parameter shape** — would mask config drift.

- **Bishop's `BracketByeSeed = "__bye__"` sentinel for odd-seed-count tournament rounds.** `AdvanceMatchAsync` short-circuits when bye seed encountered in prior round and propagates human opponent's seed forward without a match. Schema note (W14 forward-note): `TournamentMatch` has NO `MatchSlot` column — W13 derives slot locally by `(RoundNumber, Seed)` match against prior round's `BracketRecord` rows; W14 add-column migration `Phase_K_W14_TournamentMatchSlot` collapses O(N) scan to O(1) lookup.

- **W13 fire-and-forget broadcast convention.** Every `SendAsync` from a synchronous-style event handler wraps the returned `Task` with the `FireBroadcast` helper to observe exceptions via `task.ContinueWith(t => { if (t.IsFaulted) _log.LogWarning(t.Exception, "..."); }, TaskScheduler.Default)` — prevents unobserved-task finalizer firing on GC-collection of faulted task (well-known .NET gotcha when broadcasting from synchronous-style event handlers). Documented `docs/commentary-cost-admin-hub.md §3`.

- **W13 Redis sorted-set sliding-window rate-limiter pattern.** `ZREMRANGEBYSCORE key 0 (now - windowSeconds)` → `ZCARD key` → `ZADD key now now` + `EXPIRE key windowSeconds` single-batch RTT. Preferred over W12 in-memory limiter for multi-instance deployments; W12 limiter remains the fallback on `RedisException`. Preserves W12 `X-RateLimit-Limit`/`X-RateLimit-Remaining`/`X-RateLimit-Reset` + `Retry-After` header contract.

- **W13 audit-always contract for signed JWTs.** Spectator handoff sets the precedent: every JWT mint writes a `SpectatorHandoffAuditRecord` BEFORE returning the response; failure to audit short-circuits with HTTP 500. Future signed-JWT mints (replay-livestream tokens, admin-tooling tokens) follow the same audit-before-respond shape with HTTP-500-on-audit-failure.

- **W13 quarterly JWT rotation rehearsal cadence (NOT monthly).** Apone chose quarterly over W12 monthly recommendation — rationale: rehearsal exercises in-cluster Redis JWKS cache (24-hour TTL); running monthly would not exercise cache-miss path, running quarterly statistically guarantees cache-miss + refresh on most runs. Cron `0 2 1 */3 *` (02:00 UTC on 1st of every 3rd month aligned to PT calendar quarters). Documented `docs/jwt-rotation-rehearsal.md §4`. **W12 GA-ready recommendation REALISED.**

- **W13 kustomize v5.4.3 fieldSpecs `kind:` filter IGNORED bug.** Apone empirical repro: `kustomize build` ignores `kind:` filter in `commonLabels.fieldSpecs` and applies common label to ALL resources regardless of declared filter. Workaround = inverse `PatchTransformer` removal of `/metadata/namespace` from ClusterRoleBinding + ClusterRole resources applied AFTER W12 namespace transformer; cluster-scoped resources stay un-namespaced, namespaced resources keep W12 `mahjong-prod` namespace. **W13 convention: kustomize feature-flag bugs are handled by inverse transformers (post-hoc cleanup) rather than forking kustomize or waiting for upstream fix; workaround documented in dedicated `docs/<feature>-<bug>.md` doc so future agents find rationale via filename grep.**

- **W13 "PR-ready not-wired" pattern.** Apone deliverables depending on Stephen-side infrastructure (ESO secret population, TF apply, etc.) ship as committed-but-unwired patches that the W14 wire-up commit references. `infra/k8s/overlays/prod/redis-envfrom-required-patch.yaml` is the W13 paradigm exemplar — NOT in the `kustomization.yaml` `patchesStrategicMerge:` list.

### Scribe observations

1. **W14 establishes "PR-ready commented-out pre-wire" as the
   canonical W13-evolution forward-stage shape.** Apone D3 Redis
   envFrom in `kustomization.yaml` is the canonical example —
   committed-but-disabled state with a 4-line block such that the W15
   wire-up commit is a 30-second-reviewable diff. The W13 "patch on
   disk but not in `kustomization.yaml`" predecessor required
   cross-file diffing to verify the wire-up.

2. **Bishop's 7-endpoint surface family establishes 3 simultaneous
   conventions:** pagination shape uniformity (`{items, count, skip,
   limit, pageSize}` with 50/50/25 default + 200/200/100 max),
   auth-precedence ladder (`401 → 403 → 503 → 400 → 200` with 503
   inserted between 403 and 400 for store-unavailability distinct from
   bad-input), and forward-stable array-not-map schemas (`byModel[]`
   single-entry array for multi-provider widening).

3. **JWKS `rollback-rejected` introduces a new security primitive
   class.** Boundary `iat == RotationStartUtc` rejected defensively
   closes a replay-of-just-rotated-token race window. Error code
   exported as `JwtValidationService.ErrorRollbackRejected = "rollback-rejected"`
   public const extends W13 audit-always `audit-failed` precedent —
   security-rejection error codes are kebab-case constants exported
   from the validating service.

4. **`SignalRSequenceMetrics` Prometheus 3-counter family extends the
   W13 `commentary_cost_dollars_total` labelled-counter precedent.**
   Result-label constants `hit` / `miss` / `expired` exported as
   `public const string` from the metric-owning singleton for
   assertion stability and `grep`-discoverability.

5. **W14 three-renderer-big intentional hold-line at 406,635 B = +0
   is a NEW class of bundle-ledger event.** First non-decreasing
   wave since W7; breaks 8-wave monotonic ledger by design to free
   Hicks's renderer-lane bandwidth for Phase L renderer-spike Go-decision
   pre-work. **Convention established:** intentional non-decrease wave
   is a deliberate signal (must be documented with
   forward-bandwidth-redirection rationale), not a regression. W6 → W14
   cumulative −44.9 % preserved.

6. **W14 is the first wave to emit Phase L pre-work artifacts across
   3 lanes simultaneously.** `phase-l-devops-readiness.md` (Apone) +
   `phase-l-bringup.md` (Bishop) + `phase-l-renderer-spike.md` (Hicks)
   together seed the Phase L narrative. Convention: next-phase pre-work
   lands as cross-lane artifact set 1-2 waves before phase boundary.
   W15 likely sees Apone's Phase L L1 design memo pulling these into
   a unified plan.

7. **JWT rehearsal #3 GA-confirmation requires 3 rehearsal runs within
   timing-noise band.** W11 5:42 → W12 3:48 (large improvement) → W14
   3:51 (+3 s; noise band). First real prod rotation recommended for
   end-of-January 2027 paired with Q1 2027 rehearsal — establishes
   **quarterly cadence as canonical** for production JWT rotation
   operations.

8. **Vasquez visual-regression spec fix + Hicks runtime-API capture
   path together permanently resolve the W12-introduced `about:blank`
   relative-URL 404 latent bug.** Two complementary mitigations:
   `page.goto('/')` BEFORE `page.setContent(…)` for specs needing
   relative-URL HTML (Vasquez), and Playwright runtime API
   side-channel avoiding `page.setContent` entirely (Hicks). Both
   documented at `docs/test-architecture.md §5.2` and
   `docs/frontend-pwa-audit.md §14` respectively.

9. **DbSerial migration closes the W12 25-class audit at 23-of-25 +
   2-tracked = 25/25 accountable.** 2 remaining W9 Bishop-lane
   candidates identified with **3-step escalation ladder** (Bishop
   W15 → Vasquez W15 re-prompt → W16 Coordinator-direct via §4.3).
   Convention: multi-wave migration completion memo identifies
   trailing files with explicit escalation path.

10. **First-since-W11 0-violation no-amendment wave at W14 confirms
    that the W11 shared-files (`shims_shared` + `pwa_audit_workflow_shared`)
    + W13 shared-by-pipeline-artifact (`bundle_health_workflow_shared`
